West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 100
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. At Behind House, 'open window' then 'west' recorded Kitchen +10. Do this first, before any side exploration.
2. 'take lantern', 'push rug', 'open trap', 'down' recorded Cellar +25. Turn the lantern on at arrival and confirm it is lit.
3. Before descending, check inventory for lantern and sword. Take any visible sword first, and treat any treasure as keep-only.
4. Judge actions only by observed score changes. Ignore planner estimates such as +825, +45 or +20.
5. Examine the Kitchen sack and bottle seen on the table. Known objects are a better frontier than rooms already visited.
6. Choose ONE new frontier per goal. Leave any room visited twice, such as Cellar or Troll, and do not return.
7. Leaving the Troll room on first sight avoided harm. Stay out until inventory shows a sword.
8. Once you hold a treasure, keep it. A prior 'take art' recorded +4. Deposit it only along an observed route.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Do not enter the Troll room without a sword in inventory. Five entries ended in a -10 knockout and the episode ended.
2. Do not shuttle Cellar<->Troll. About ten moves gave zero score and no new information. Leave after the second visit.
3. Do not follow replayed historical goals (bar, egg) that have no verified route. They produced only cycling.
4. Do not use risky verbs such as 'jump' at edges. An earlier run recorded -10 death that way.
5. Do not drop a scored treasure away from a verified deposit spot. The painting was dropped twice in the Gallery earlier.
6. Do not spend opening steps on the mailbox, leaflet or North House. They scored nothing again.
7. Do not repeat a move after your own note says it is hostile or blocked. 'Troll blocks all exits' preceded another 'north'.
8. Do not plan on returning home underground. The trap door barred shut behind the descent, and no return route is observed.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

Historian episode review — episode 37
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 8 positive + 8 negative (configured per category: 8)

Strongest decisions came early: 'west' from Behind House recorded Kitchen +10, 'down' from the Living Room recorded +25 on entering the Cellar, and 'take art' in the Gallery recorded +4. Final score was 29. After that, the Actor stalled for hundreds of steps. It cycled Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio, entered Troll four times and retreated each time, and chased the stale 'take bar' and 'take egg' goals with no verified route. Nothing in the evidence shows an inventory check before the Troll entries. The lantern was apparently failing ('more light than from the brass lantern'), yet the Actor waited, closed and opened the bottle, then went 'north' in the dark and a grue killed it (-10). The planner's repeated stale goals made things worse, but the Actor's cycling and dark-room risk were its own. Next time: check inventory and light first, and use a different exit from any room already entered twice.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. At Behind House, 'open window' then 'west' recorded Kitchen +10. Do this first, before any side exploration.
2. In the Living Room, take sword and lantern, check 'inventory', then 'down'. That 'down' recorded Cellar +25.
3. 'take art' in the Gallery recorded +4. Keep any scored treasure in inventory and do not drop it away from a verified deposit spot.
4. Turn the lantern on at Cellar arrival and confirm it is lit. Re-check light before any exit into an unlit room.
5. Retreating 'south' from the Troll room each time avoided harm. Stay out until inventory confirms a sword.
6. Set one new frontier per goal. After a second entry into any room, take a different exit instead of the one just used.
7. Trust only recorded score events. Ignore historical +20 or +45 estimates that lack a verified route from your current room.
8. When text warns about light, fix it first: check the lantern, or return to a lit room. Never push on.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Do not move into a dark room. 'north' from East Chasm with a failing lantern ended in a grue death (-10).
2. Do not shuttle Cellar<->East Chasm or Gallery<->Studio. Dozens of moves there gave zero score. Pick an unvisited exit after the second visit.
3. Do not follow replayed 'take bar' or 'take egg' goals. They have no verified route and produced only cycling.
4. Do not enter the Troll room without 'inventory' showing a sword. It was entered four times with no check.
5. Do not 'wait' or toggle the bottle while lost in the dark. Those actions cost steps and fixed nothing about the light.
6. Do not give items to the thief or shuffle the manual and bottle. Those actions scored nothing.
7. Never use 'jump' or other risky verbs at edges or in dark places. An earlier run recorded a death that way.
8. Do not spend more than a few steps without new information. Over 300 steps after the +4 gained nothing; try a fresh frontier early.
